### BATCH_005 · Photography canon at depth
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/BATCH_005_CHUNKS.jsonl` (161 chunks, 275 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-18
**Source corpus:** 32 extracted sources from `raw/02_TIER_1_CANON_BOOKS/photography/`, `raw/09_ART_SERIES/`, `raw/10_REFERENCE/photography_scans/`, and `raw/PHOTOGRPAHY GOLD BOOKS VIDEOS EVERYTHING /`

The first deep photography corpus in the SNIPED knowledge base. Where BATCH_001 sampled two photographer studies (Leibovitz, Haas), BATCH_005 installs the full canonical theory layer plus the 9-photographer SNIPED Art Series doctrine plus supplementary scholarly material. Four priority tiers:

- **Pri 1 · canonical theory + craft (9 sources · 86 chunks):** Sontag *On Photography* (the noeme corollary · camera as appropriation · 12 chunks), Barthes *Camera Lucida* (the noeme · "that-has-been" · 10 chunks), Day *Robert Frank's The Americans* (personal documentary lineage · 10 chunks), Shore *The Nature of Photographs* (the three-level analysis: physical / depictive / mental · 8 chunks), Szarkowski *William Eggleston's Guide* (institutional legitimation of color · 6 chunks), Freeman *The Photographer's Eye* (composition vocabulary · 10 chunks), Freeman *The Photographer's Vision* (visual literacy · 10 chunks), Stevens / Aronson *Avedon: Something Personal* (studio-as-apparatus · 12 chunks), Maisel *Light, Gesture, and Color* (the three things · 8 chunks).
- **Pri 2 · SNIPED Art Series doctrine (19 sources · 65 chunks):** 9 Art_Series production plans (Avedon, Eggleston, Leibovitz, Shore, Herzog, Frank, Meyerowitz, Iturbide, Haas · 27 chunks), 9 paired Studies (36 chunks), 1 Art Series wrapper (2 chunks). The full operator-voice canon of the SNIPED-internal photographer doctrine is now chunk-addressable.
- **Pri 3 · scanned theory + transcript (1 source · 3 chunks):** Abloh *Core Studio Public Lecture* (tourist mode · 3% rule · cheat codes).
- **Pri 4 · GOLD vault text-dense (3 sources · 7 chunks):** Dubiel · Avedon *American West* + Sartre (existential portraiture frame · 3 chunks), Talbot · *Fred Herzog: Modern Color* (the restrained-color middle register · 2 chunks), *Art Book 1994* review of Frank *The Americans* (the monograph form · 2 chunks).

**Domain distribution (13 domains):** art-series 65 · photography-theory 19 · aesthetics 16 · portraiture 14 · documentary 12 · composition 12 · visual-literacy 8 · taste 7 · ethics 2 · color 2 · operator-doctrine 2 · sequencing 1 · client-application 1. The `ethics` domain emerged from the Sontag non-intervention + democratic suspension chunks; operator approved keeping it as a 13th domain rather than re-tagging.

**New canon installed (12 cluster-level loadings):** Sontag's appropriation ethics, Barthes's noeme, Day's personal documentary lineage, Shore's three-level analysis, Szarkowski's color legitimation, Freeman's composition + vision dyad, Stevens's studio-as-apparatus, Maisel's three things, the 9-photographer SNIPED Art Series in operator voice, the Avedon-Sartre existential frame, Herzog's restrained-color middle register, and the Frank monograph form.

**Cross-references opened:** `intel_pricing_logic.md` (Stevens-Avedon as the case study Enns abstracts), `intel_perennial_logic.md` (Day, Dubiel, Talbot, Art Book 1994 all provide structural validation), `intel_hospitality_layer.md` (Sontag non-intervention names what hospitality structurally corrects), `intel_ai_sentiment.md` (Sontag + Barthes + Abloh ground the hybrid-operator stance), `feedback_lineage_doctrine.md` (Day + Abloh name the lineage position productively), `feedback_visual_direction_luxury_editorial.md` (Sontag + Szarkowski + Freeman + Maisel + Talbot defend the locked aesthetic from theory + craft + lineage angles).

### BATCH_006 · Operator-engine skill layer
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/BATCH_006_CHUNKS.jsonl` (114 chunks, 280 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-19
**Source corpus:** 108 sources across 3 raw/ folders · `raw/_skills/` (50 SNIPED skill packs + 1 build-queue meta-doc), `raw/Claude_AI_Skills_50_Upload_Ready (1)/` (50 framework prompt packs), `raw/10_REFERENCE/_intake_2026-05-18/` (4 Claude/AI tool workflows + 1 framework primitive + 2 JSON automation blueprints).

The operator-engine skill layer. Where BATCH_001-004 captured the SNIPED operating spine and BATCH_005 installed the photography canon, BATCH_006 makes the skill primitives themselves chunk-addressable. The result: every SNIPED skill is invokable by name and INVOKE-WHEN condition, every external framework prompt is searchable as a 1-chunk pack, and the supporting Claude Code / n8n / Remotion tooling primitives are reference-ready.

**This is not a general AI-course dump.** Every chunk in BATCH_006 strengthens the system's ability to execute SNIPED workflows. Course material, AI-canon books, transcripts, and web-codebase docs were explicitly held out for BATCH_008 or other downstream batches. Four priority tiers:

- **Pri 1 · SNIPED skill-build queue meta-doc (1 source · 1 chunk):** The 5-rule discipline that governs every SNIPED skill (one per source, reference don't duplicate, frontmatter is load-bearing, MANDATORY READING in every skill, refuse drift).
- **Pri 1 · SNIPED skills (50 sources · 53 chunks):** All 50 `sniped-*` skill packs · canonical-truths, direction-stack, hospitality-layer, partnership-protocol, monday-cockpit, execution-prioritization, lean-audit, assistant-task-routing, 10 production-sop skills (pre-shoot, shoot-day-reset, shoot-day-strategic-free, post-shoot-same-day, capture-to-delivery, retoucher-onboarding, production-os, pixieset-gallery, post-delivery, lightroom-rails), 4 outreach-sop skills (vib-outreach, caption-writer, discovery-to-close, notion-crm-update), 3 pricing/positioning skills (pricing-decision, wwp-positioning, partnership-protocol), 1 client-application skill (art-series), 8 aesthetics skills (luxury-edit, hero-composite-ceiling, hero-composite-lite, evoto-skin-pass, higgsfield-pipeline, seedream-prompt, lighting-vault, ai-image-tool-pick), 2 ai-tooling skills (ai-image-tool-pick, udemy-ai-accelerants), 14 strategy intel skills, with the 3 longest (caption-writer, vib-outreach, luxury-edit) carrying an extended-methodology second chunk.

**Source coverage:**
- **The Big Payback** (Charnas · 8 + 2 synthesis): hip-hop became global culture; built by entrepreneurs/hustlers; ownership-vs-being-owned; distribution as the leverage point; credibility as currency; crossover without losing the base; exploitation/appropriation cautions; the artist-as-brand/mogul.
- **Dilla Time** (Charnas · 7 + 1 synthesis): the third-path rhythmic invention; mastery through obsessive private practice; constraints producing the signature; influence without fame; the Detroit scene/mentors; document-from-inside; feel over precision.
- **Decoded** (Jay-Z · 6 + 1 synthesis): rap as layered poetry; re-create yourself; the hustler's transferable logic; the persona/mask; Marcy-as-origin-material; the recording as deliberate transformation.
- **The Autobiography of Gucci Mane** (6): deliberate post-prison reinvention; prolific output as the comeback engine; honest accounting; family inheritance; Atlanta scene-building; the name as a built asset.
- **Hurricanes** (Rick Ross · 5 + 1 synthesis): persona-construction; the authenticity (image-vs-fact) question; the birth of the artist; transcending setbacks; taking a name and mythology.
- **Empire State of Mind** (Greenburg · 5): street corner to corner office; building a notorious brand; equity over fees; diversification on one brand; the come-up grounded in Marcy.
- **Supreme Models** (Reynolds · 3 · LIGHT, image-heavy): the visual archive as cultural memory; being-first as inheritance; image as authorship and self-definition.
- **Cross-source synthesis** (5): self-authorship is the through-line; ownership over being owned; persona is a built asset; craft discipline + reps make the legend; document from inside the lineage.

**Supreme Models received light coverage** (3 chunks) because it is an image-heavy coffee-table book (244 images / ~30k words of profiles); it is cross-listed in `photography/` (md5-identical · chunked once).

**Held / excluded (0 chunks):** the **Status pair** (The Status Game · Storr, and Status and Culture · Marx) is HELD for a future `CULTURE_AND_STATUS` lane (status sociology, distinct from the primary-source lineage focus); the **`memoirs_biographies/` folder** (~16 general founder/media biographies · Branson, Schultz, Rockefeller, Vreeland, Coddington, etc.) is HELD for a separate future biography/founder/media lane; **The Tanning of America and The Song Machine were excluded** because BATCH_002 already chunked them.

### N8N_AUTOMATION_SYSTEMS · 6 n8n workflows mini-batch (the automation IMPLEMENTATION layer)
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/N8N_AUTOMATION_SYSTEMS_CHUNKS.jsonl` (18 chunks · ~33 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-19
**Source corpus:** 6 n8n workflow JSON exports (The AI Edge) at `raw/10_REFERENCE/_intake_2026-05-19/automations/` · 2 voice-agent + 4 prompt-engineer-agent. Extracted via stdlib `json` (no new dependencies); credential values, auth tokens, webhook secrets, and `pinData` were stripped (credentials reduced to name-only references); literal-secret scan CLEAN.

The workflow-architecture layer that connects the B2B_POSITIONING_CLAUDE_OPERATOR responsiveness-AI demand and the OPPORTUNITY_MANAGEMENT_TEMPLATES opportunity-card / business-case intake to implementable n8n builds. It is the 5th 2026-05-19 mini-batch and the supply-side implementation end of the demand-to-delivery spine (B2B demand -> OMT intake/ROI/readiness -> N8N build). It extends the BATCH_006 `automation-blueprint` domain (6 -> 17 chunks). 18 chunks across 4 domains (automation-blueprint 11 · ai-tooling 4 · operator-process 2 · client-application 1 · no new domains).

**Two workflow clusters:**
- **Cluster A · voice-agent automation (2 workflows):** AI Phone Call Assistant (VAPI · 17 nodes) + n8n & RetellAI (RetellAI · 23 nodes). Webhook/form trigger -> langchain agent (gpt-4.1-mini) with a Perplexity research tool + an Airtable data tool -> respondToWebhook; an outbound httpRequest triggers the provider call API. RetellAI adds a post-call follow-up branch (Status-of-Call switch -> store report -> follow-up LLM gated by a structured-output parser). Both share a 3-step provider scaffold (connect provider / activities webhook / trigger call). This IS the AI-voice-receptionist / missed-call-text-back category.
- **Cluster B · prompt-engineer agent (4 workflows):** chat-input + form-submission entries -> one master agent (OpenRouter) routing to two toolWorkflow sub-agents; deep-reasoning + normal model-tier sub-workflows (executeWorkflowTrigger) each with a model-selector chain. The n8n implementation of a prompt-engineering agent.

**The 18 chunks:** 5 per-workflow architecture (001 VAPI · 002 RetellAI+follow-up · 003 chat orchestrator · 004 form orchestrator · 005 deep-reasoning vs normal model tiers) + 13 cross-cutting patterns (006 voice-agent provider scaffold · 007 agent + tool-node pattern · 008 trigger-logic taxonomy · 009 multi-entry orchestration · 010 model-tier routing · 011 sub-workflow-as-tool composition · 012 structured-output guardrail · 013 IF/switch branching · 014 Airtable-as-memory-by-database · 015 httpRequest + respondToWebhook action layer · 016 credential-reference architecture · 017 Perplexity research-tool integration · 018 opportunity-card -> n8n-build bridge).

**MCP / memory note:** no dedicated MCP node and no dedicated memory node are present; chunk 014 documents Airtable-as-memory-by-database as the closest analog (not overclaimed).

**Security:** credential values, auth tokens, API keys, bearer-token values, webhook secrets, and `pinData` were stripped at extraction. Credential references are name-only (`airtableTokenApi/The AI Edge`, `openAiApi/Ops Management`, `openAiApi/The AI Edge`, `perplexityApi/The AI Edge`, `openRouterApi/Prompt Engineer Agent`). Literal-secret scan over all extracted files + chunks: 0 hits.

- **BATCH_006 operator skill layer:** directly EXTENDS the `automation-blueprint` domain · the voice agents (001-002, 006) are the n8n cousins of the B6 ElevenLabs voice-agent blueprint; the agent + tool-node primitive (007) and sub-workflow-as-tool composition (011) mirror the `.claude/skills/` single-responsibility design.

**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/LITERARY_CANON_DYSTOPIAN_CHUNKS.jsonl` (17 chunks · ~28 KB)

Extracted via stdlib `zipfile`+HTML-strip (epub) + `ebook-convert` (mobi) + `pdftotext -layout` (pdf) · no OCR · no new dependencies · 161,792 words.

The dystopian / systems-warning pass · the cautionary counterweight (the do-not-build conscience) to the corpus's AI / automation / operator-build layers. It is the 8th mini-batch and the 2nd literary lane, and **registers `systems-thinking` as the corpus's 60th unique domain** (distinct from the older, broader `systems` bucket). 17 chunks across 4 domains (systems-thinking NEW 8 · operator-doctrine 4 · culture 3 · ethics 2 · strategy not used).

**IMPORTANT · Brave New World Revisited is Huxley's 1958 NONFICTION essays, not the novel Brave New World** (1932, not staged). It is chunked as the systems-warning nonfiction it is, and is the single most on-theme source for the operator-warning brief.

**Signal captured:** propaganda, surveillance, language control, social conditioning, state/corporate power, fear-as-governance, comfort-as-control, attention discipline, truth decay, human dignity under systems pressure, institutional design, and the warnings for operators building AI / automation systems.

**Source coverage:**
- **Animal Farm** (Orwell · 5 chunks): the revolution betrayed (pigs become men), propaganda + rewritten rules ("more equal than others"), Squealer's spin that erodes memory, Boxer's exploited loyalty, the dogs as the force behind the slogans.
- **The Handmaid's Tale** (Atwood · 5 chunks): theocratic reduction of person to function, language control (renaming / ritual greetings / banned reading), surveillance + informants (the Eyes), gradual normalization (the slope not the cliff), complicity of co-opted enforcers + private resistance + dignity ("Nolite te bastardes carborundorum").
- **Brave New World Revisited** (Huxley nonfiction · 5 chunks): over-organization, propaganda in a democratic society (manufacturing consent vs informing), the arts of selling + comfort/distraction as control (soma), brainwashing/conditioning, and the antidote · education for freedom.
- **Cross-text synthesis** (2 chunks): the dystopian warnings as the operator's do-not-build checklist; Orwell's boot/fear vs Huxley's soma/comfort (Postman's distinction) · modern AI/attention systems fail the Huxleyan way.

**Study guides absent:** the 1984 SparkNotes and Fahrenheit 451 Bloom's Critical Interpretations are NOT in the lane (orphaned secondaries · their primaries were not staged) · 0 chunks · kept skipped.

**New domain:** `systems-thinking` (8 chunks) is the corpus's **60th unique domain** · institutional-design and control-mechanism analysis · distinct from the older `systems` bucket.

**Copyright-safe quote discipline:** in-copyright texts · `direct_quotes` are SHORT illustrative lines only (longest 27 words · a sentence or two · fair-use scale) · no long passages reproduced.

**Cross-references opened to existing corpus:**

- **BATCH_006 operator skill layer:** the cautionary counterweight · B6 teaches how to BUILD agentic systems, this lane warns what they can become (surveillance, conditioning, attention capture).
- **BATCH_007 operator doctrine:** fear-as-governance + comfort-as-control are anti-patterns to the SNIPED hospitality + dignity stance; gradual normalization (009) sharpens the bright-line / operating-locks discipline.
- **N8N_AUTOMATION_SYSTEMS:** the most direct tie · surveillance + data tables (N8N 014), AI voice agents + attention capture (N8N 001-002), automated persuasion are exactly Orwell's and Huxley's subjects · the N8N structured-output guardrail + human-approval gate (012/013) is the practical answer.
- **PROMPT_TEMPLATES_DEEP:** language control (the rewritten commandments · Gilead's renaming) is the dark mirror of prompt-craft as language shaping · the ethical dimension.
- **Future BATCH_008 AI/tech canon (NOT started):** the ethical / cautionary counterweight to read the AI-builder canon against · Huxley's propaganda + conditioning + chemical-persuasion warnings apply almost directly to AI recommendation and engagement-optimization systems. Cross-reference, do not merge.

**Domain distribution (1 NEW domain · systems-thinking):** systems-thinking 8 · operator-doctrine 4 · culture 3 · ethics 2.

**Source coverage:**
- **Ulysses** (Joyce · 6 chunks · 001-006): the ordinary day made epic (total attention), stream of consciousness, Leopold Bloom's everyday decency/empathy, Dublin rendered from total specificity, Molly's "yes" as affirmation, and language as the hero (maximalist craft).
- **Slaughterhouse-Five** (Vonnegut · 5 chunks · 007-011): anti-war witness (the Children's Crusade), "So it goes" grief without flinching, Dresden / bearing witness to the unspeakable, "unstuck in time" (trauma rendered structurally), and absurdity + dark humor as survival.
- **Lolita** (Nabokov · 5 chunks · 012-016 · craft/moral/literary level only · no graphic content): the unreliable narrator, the seduction of style, the reader's manufactured complicity, beauty-vs-morality (style divorced from ethics), and language as both art and concealment · the boundary stone that gorgeous style must never launder substance.
- **The Kite Runner** (Hosseini · 5 chunks · 017-021): guilt + the long arc of atonement, "For you, a thousand times over" (loyalty/love), the wound that shapes a life, exile + displacement, and fathers-and-sons / secret kinship (inheritance).
- **As a Man Thinketh** (Allen · 4 chunks · 022-025): thought shapes character, the mind as a garden, circumstance reveals the man, and calm strength / self-control · the 1903 root of PERSONAL_OPERATING_CODE 009 (mindset-as-software).
- **The Prophet** (Gibran · 4 chunks · 026-029): work is love made visible, on children (not yours to possess), joy and sorrow are inseparable, and the parable form (image over argument).
- **Cross-author synthesis** (3 chunks · 030-032): literature as attention training, moral seriousness + craft as the anti-shallow-content standard, and operator lessons taken as formation NOT shallow takeaways.

**The Prophet `.lit` conversion:** the conditional resolved POSITIVE · `ebook-convert` on the Microsoft Reader `.lit` succeeded (12,924 words · above the 3,000-word floor) · The Prophet is INCLUDED with 4 chunks.

### CULTURE_AND_STATUS · status / culture / symbolic-value theory mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/CULTURE_AND_STATUS_CHUNKS.jsonl` (16 chunks · ~30 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-22
**Source corpus:** 2 books from `raw/03_TIER_2_CANON_BOOKS/persuasion_psych/` · Will Storr, The Status Game (2021), and W. David Marx, Status and Culture (2022) · the held Status pair deferred from BATCH_009 and BATCH_010. Extracted via stdlib zipfile+HTML-strip (epub, spine-ordered) · no OCR · no new dependencies · 282,609 words.

The status / culture theory layer · it connects symbolic value, group belonging, taste, hierarchy, cultural capital, and social performance to SNIPED brand and image positioning. It is the 11th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · it deepens the existing `status` domain (de Botton's Status Anxiety + Simler/Hanson's Elephant in the Brain from BATCH_003) with two more canonical status texts. 16 chunks across seven existing domains: status (4), culture (3), systems-thinking (3), brand-psychology (2), strategy (2), aesthetics (1), lineage (1, light).

**Signal captured:** status as a fundamental human drive, symbolic value, hierarchy, taste, identity signaling, group belonging, fashion/status mechanics, cultural capital, social performance, and how status systems shape brand, image, and SNIPED positioning.

**Source coverage:**
- **The Status Game** (Storr · 7 + 1 synthesis): status as a fundamental drive (the hidden game); the three games (dominance, virtue, success); status detected automatically from subtle signals; prestige is granted by others, not declared; when status games go bad (humiliation, tyranny); positive-sum beats zero-sum dominance; play a good game (earn status through usefulness and skill).
- **Status and Culture** (Marx · 7 + 1 synthesis): the desire for status drives taste; conventions and signals communicate rank; status is the engine of fashion cycles and constant change; subcultures as alternative status hierarchies; cultural capital as learned/inherited status; the internet flattened status signaling into ephemeral fads; costly, hard-to-fake signals carry the most status.
- **Cross-source synthesis** (2): sell status, not features (status systems shape brand and positioning); status is the operator's lever, played as a good game with low self-orientation (the ethical guardrail).

**Lineage used lightly** (1 chunk · Marx's cultural-capital / inherited-status concept) only where warranted. **No unrelated culture/status sources were included** (the BATCH_009 EXPANSION set, the memoirs_biographies folder, and the recovery/acquisition items all stayed out · 0 chunks).

**Copyright-safe quote discipline:** both in-copyright trade books · `direct_quotes` are SHORT illustrative lines only (longest 14 words) · 9 of 16 chunks carry a quote, 7 paraphrase · extracted full text is INTERNAL chunk-authoring reference only.

**Cross-references opened to existing corpus:**

- **BATCH_010 lineage + Black culture canon:** BATCH_010 shows the lived status games (the come-up, persona-construction, the boss image); CULTURE_AND_STATUS supplies the THEORY of why they work. The hip-hop memoirs are the case studies; Storr/Marx are the mechanics.
- **BATCH_009 commercial voice:** the signaling and distinction theory beneath B009's brand-psychology (Cialdini social proof, Sutherland costly-signalling, Shotton distinctiveness) · the deeper status logic under the persuasion levers.
- **LITERARY_CANON_BLACK:** the status / dignity / recognition themes (Morrison, Hurston) get their sociological frame.
- **BATCH_005 photography canon:** status signaling via image, taste-as-distinction · informs the image-as-status-signal thesis (the portrait confers status).
- **SNIPED Lineage Doctrine + `intel_status_psychology`:** the canonical-text extension of the existing status-psychology intel · two more primary status texts for pricing, premium-buyer psychology, and the founder-buyer experience.

**Domain distribution (NO NEW domain):** status 4 · culture 3 · systems-thinking 3 · brand-psychology 2 · strategy 2 · aesthetics 1 · lineage 1 (light).

**STALE-FLAG tags:** 0.

### BATCH_009_EXPANSION · commercial-strategy expansion mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/BATCH_009_EXPANSION_CHUNKS.jsonl` (22 chunks · ~33 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-23
**Source corpus:** 5 net-new books from `raw/02_TIER_1_CANON_BOOKS/sales_positioning/` · Chris Voss & Tahl Raz, Never Split the Difference (2016); Adam Morgan, Eating the Big Fish (2009); Al Ramadan/Dave Peterson/Christopher Lochhead/Kevin Maney, Play Bigger (2016); Seth Godin, Tribes (2008); Christensen/Dillon/Hall/Duncan, Competing Against Luck (2016) · the operator-deferred BATCH_009 EXPANSION set. Extracted via stdlib zipfile+HTML-strip (epub) and pdftotext (pdf) · no OCR · no new dependencies · 404,437 words.

The commercial-strategy expansion layer **after the BATCH_009 commercial-voice core**. Where BATCH_009 core is the copy/persuasion/offer craft (Hopkins, Cialdini, Hormozi, Trout, Dunford), this is the strategic-commercial layer above the copy: how to create and frame a market and close the deal. It is the 12th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · 22 chunks across eleven existing domains: brand-psychology (4), strategy (4), sales-flow (3), positioning (2), systems-thinking (2), content-strategy (2), brand (1), commercial-architecture (1), operator-process (1), client-application (1), offer-design (1).

**Signal captured:** negotiation, challenger positioning, category design, tribe building, customer progress (jobs-to-be-done), demand creation, strategic offers, and market framing.

**Source coverage:**
- **Never Split the Difference** (Voss · 4 · negotiation): tactical empathy and the late-night FM DJ voice; mirroring, labeling, and the accusation audit; "no" is protection and "that's right" is the breakthrough; calibrated how/what questions, Ackerman bargaining, and Black Swans.
- **Eating the Big Fish** (Morgan · 4 · challenger positioning): the ideas-led (not budget-led) challenger mindset; the Lighthouse Identity; Sacrifice and Overcommitment to concentrate force; intelligent naivety and thought leadership of the consumer.
- **Play Bigger** (Ramadan et al. · 5 + 1 synthesis · category design): design the category you can lead and the category king's economics; lead with a Point of View; the Magic Triangle (product/company/category); the Lightning Strike; conditioning the market.
- **Tribes** (Godin · 3 · tribe building): a tribe needs a shared interest and a way to communicate; leadership is not management and heretics challenge the status quo; lead the smallest viable tribe and give them tools to connect.

- **Money, Wealth & Getting Ahead** (SNIPED synthesis · 3 + synthesis): constraint elimination + correct-contrarian bets; the Protected Hour + 60%-beats-zero + compounding systems; wealth = passive income above burn rate (else "a job with better branding").
- **Cross-source synthesis** (2): the capital-thinking stack (own, do not only serve) + the optionality guardrail (capital thinking is a lens, not a pivot).

It is the 18th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · it is anchored on the existing `founder-psychology`. 12 chunks across nine domains: founder-psychology (2 · anchor), operator-process (2), operator-doctrine (2), strategy (1), brand (1), commercial-architecture (1), systems-thinking (1), culture (1), ethics (1).

**Signal captured:** founder return, repairing drift, simplification, operational renewal, cultural repair, brand trust, customer experience, standards, focus, emotional resilience, and how an operator diagnoses what broke after growth.

**Pour Your Heart Into It remains the already-canonical BUILD arc in FOUNDER_SECOND_TIER (2 chunks · 1987-1997 origin).** Onward is the distinct turnaround/repair COMPANION (2008-2011, Schultz's return). Different book, different period, different lesson set (build vs repair) · no content overlap.

**Source coverage (the turnaround arc):**
- **Diagnosis (systems-thinking):** post-scale decline is systemic and incremental (the loose-thread / commoditization drift), not one failure · name the root cause before fixing.
- **Founder return (founder-psychology):** re-taking hands-on daily control to repair what you built; founder identity as both fuel and hazard.
- **Standards reset (operator-process):** closing all 7,100 US stores to retrain espresso · a costly, visible quality reset, not a memo.
- **Brand-trust repair (brand):** restoring the experience (the romance, the third place, craft and aroma) · brand lives in texture, not the logo.
- **Transformation Agenda (strategy):** a named, forward-leaning framework sequenced from the burning platform; honesty about problems plus concrete action.
- **Focus by subtraction (operator-doctrine):** slowing openings, closing ~600 underperformers · the discipline of saying no to the growth reflex.
- **Innovate from the core (commercial-architecture):** VIA and the rewards platform · new revenue only when relevant to the core and values.
- **Plan B (operator-process):** ~$400M permanent structural cost cuts via Lean and rebuilt unit economics, while protecting customer-facing investment.
- **Cultural repair (culture):** the New Orleans leadership conference · reconnecting partners in person after cuts · trust as load-bearing infrastructure.
- **Accountability + resilience (founder-psychology):** owning missteps publicly while holding conviction through the first quarterly loss and media scrutiny.
- **Conscience (ethics):** fighting for the company's life without losing its soul · protecting health care, ethical sourcing, and community even while cutting.
- **Synthesis (012):** the repeatable repair sequence (diagnose root cause, re-take control, reset standards, focus by subtraction, structural economics, cultural/trust repair, core-adjacent innovation, protected values) + the explicit optionality guardrail.

**CURRENT_OPERATOR_REALITY_BRIEF respected (current-state anchor · NOT chunked doctrine).** All 12 chunks reference the brief and hold the turnaround as a pattern-library lens against current reality (solo operator, early ideation/build mode, not recovering a scaled company).

**Identity optionality preserved:** turnaround patterns are decision-support / pattern-library lenses, **NOT a directive that BJ return to old SNIPED Media or revive an old brand** · Schultz's repair-a-mature-company arc is the OPPOSITE of BJ's early-build stage, so the value is the diagnostic discipline, not the act of reviving · this lane does NOT finalize SNIPED, SNIPED Media, or BASEPLATE direction · photography remains one option among several. Chunk 012 makes the optionality discipline explicit.

**No new domain.** All 9 domains pre-exist; `founder-psychology` is the anchor. The plan's two conditional domains (culture, ethics) were both warranted and included.

### PERSUASION_RECOVERY · recovered behavioral-economics primary source mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/PERSUASION_RECOVERY_CHUNKS.jsonl` (15 chunks · ~28 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-24
**Source corpus:** 1 recovered behavioral-economics book from `raw/03_TIER_2_CANON_BOOKS/persuasion_psych/` (the `_RECOVERED` epub staged in RECOVERY_STAGING_PASS · superseding the old unsupported djvu) · Predictably Irrational: The Hidden Forces That Shape Our Decisions, Revised and Expanded Edition (Dan Ariely). Extracted via ebook-convert · no OCR · no new dependencies · ~107,956 words.

The **recovered behavioral-economics primary source** that sits beneath the applied-persuasion canon (BATCH_009 / ADVERTISING_RECOVERY). It is the 23rd mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 9 domains pre-exist · `decision-making` anchors (a thin domain at count 1, grows by 4 to 5). 15 chunks across: decision-making (4 · anchor), offer-design (2), pricing (2), strategy (2), sales-flow (1), commercial-architecture (1), brand-psychology (1), ethics (1), operator-doctrine (1).

**Signal captured:** irrational decision patterns, relativity, arbitrary coherence / price anchoring, the power of FREE, social vs market norms, the hot-cold empathy gap, procrastination + self-control, the endowment effect, keeping options open, the effect of expectations, the placebo of price, and the honesty / fudge factor, held as a decision-support lens for ethical offer design, fair pricing, customer behavior, and operator judgment.

**Source coverage (single-source attribution · Ariely):**
- **Relativity (Ch1):** we judge value by comparison, not absolutely (the cheap-pen / expensive-suit asymmetry) · the decoy / asymmetric-dominance effect (the Economist subscription).
- **Arbitrary coherence / anchoring (Ch2):** the social-security-number auction · the first arbitrary price anchors all later willingness to pay (Tom Sawyer / Assael pearls).
- **The cost of zero cost (Ch3):** FREE is an emotional trigger that distorts demand (Amazon free shipping · Hershey's Kiss).
- **Social vs market norms (Ch4):** introducing money breaks a social exchange and is hard to undo · the business-design flip side (run loyalty on social norms, consistently).
- **The hot-cold empathy gap (Ch5)** + **procrastination/self-control (Ch6)** + **the endowment effect (Ch7, Duke tickets):** we underpredict emotion, need external commitment over willpower, and overvalue what we own.
- **Keeping doors open (Ch8, Xiang Yu):** we irrationally pay to preserve options; closing weak doors focuses.
- **The effect of expectations (Ch9)** + **the placebo of price (Ch10, Veladone/SoBe):** belief, presentation, and price physically change the experienced quality.
- **The context of our character (Ch11-12):** the fudge factor · honest people cheat a little, and moral reminders (not penalties) stop it.
- **Synthesis (1):** predictable irrationality is systematic (markets do not self-correct · the 2008-crisis framing · `strategy`) + behavioral economics as honest decision-support / the free-lunch design + the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

Extracted via ebook-convert · no OCR · no new dependencies · ~106,892 words.

The **recovered literary-canon layer** completing two previously-deferred slots in the existing literary lanes (Beloved was deferred at LITERARY_CANON_BLACK time as a 4-page stub; Seagull was deferred at LITERARY_CANON_GENERAL time as djvu). It is the 25th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 5 domains pre-exist · dual anchor `culture` (the literary-lane anchor) + `lineage` (Beloved · Black-lineage / ancestral memory). 14 chunks across: culture (5), operator-doctrine (3), lineage (2), ethics (2), aesthetics (2).

**Signal captured:** Beloved as cultural memory, haunting, lineage, trauma-history, and ethical witness; Jonathan Livingston Seagull as aspiration, craft, discipline, nonconformity, and the danger of turning metaphor into a belief system. Held as an interpretive / cultural pattern-library lens.

**Source coverage (per-source attribution · Beloved weighted heavier 9 vs 5):**
- **Beloved (Morrison · 8 + synthesis):** Sixty-million ancestral inheritance; rememory (memory as collective, place-bound, inescapable); the haunting (the unprocessed past returns until faced); the theft of self as slavery's deepest violence; mother-love and the impossible choice under bondage (anguished witness, not verdict); the Clearing (communal self-love as lineage-rooted resistance); claiming ownership of the freed self (liberation is only the start); form-as-witness ("not a story to pass on").
- **Jonathan Livingston Seagull (Bach · 4 + synthesis):** mastery through relentless, joyful practice; the cost of pursuing excellence beyond the flock (nonconformity); craft as its own reward (the pursuit of perfection); and the reading-discipline chunk that takes the craft/aspiration metaphor while explicitly declining to literalize the Part-Three messiah turn into a belief system or self-help doctrine.
- **Synthesis (2):** literature as the humanistic-formation counterweight to the operator/AI-build canon (cross-source · `culture`) + the interpretive-lens discipline and the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

**No new domain · `literary`/`identity`/`memory`/`trauma`/`freedom`/`myth`/`faith`/`self-help` deliberately NOT registered.** There has never been a `literary` domain; memory/trauma/freedom route to `culture` / `lineage` / `ethics`; identity to `culture` / `lineage`; myth to `culture` / `aesthetics` / `operator-doctrine`. `systems-thinking` and `mindset` were available "if warranted" but judged not strongly warranted enough to force (the lane kept to the established literary routing, avoiding the self-help register for Seagull).

**Distinguished from the existing literary canon.** Beloved vs The Bluest Eye / Morrison author-overlap (LITERARY_CANON_BLACK): Beloved is a net-new title (it was deferred there as a 4-page stub), its themes (rememory, the haunting weight of slavery, mother-love under bondage, self-ownership, form-as-witness) distinct from The Bluest Eye's beauty-standard internalization. Seagull vs As a Man Thinketh / The Prophet (LITERARY_CANON_GENERAL): net-new title (deferred as djvu), read in the same philosophical-fable register but at the craft/cultural level.

### HISTORICAL_BIOGRAPHY · Chernow's Grant + Washington mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/HISTORICAL_BIOGRAPHY_CHUNKS.jsonl` (16 chunks · ~28 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-24
**Source corpus:** 2 Ron Chernow biographies from `raw/03_TIER_2_CANON_BOOKS/memoirs_biographies/` (already repo-local · not recovery files) · Grant (epub · 477,787 words); Washington: A Life (pdf · clean text layer, not scanned · 434,269 words). Extracted via ebook-convert + pdftotext · no OCR · no new dependencies · ~912,056 words combined. **CURATED (representative), NOT exhaustive** per the scope guard.

The **curated historical-biography leadership/power lane**. It is the 26th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 7 domains pre-exist · dual anchor `leadership` (the existing BATCH_002/003 home) + `power` (the dominant theme). 16 chunks across: power (4), operator-doctrine (3), ethics (3), leadership (2), strategy (2), operator-process (1), culture (1).

**Signal captured:** command under pressure, reputation, institutional trust, coalition discipline, restraint, war leadership, political transition, ethics under ambiguity, failure recovery, the executive burden, and the difference between personal character and public myth.

**Source coverage (per-source attribution · Grant 8 / Washington 8 · roughly equal weight):**
- **Grant (Chernow · 7 + synthesis):** quiet command (earned authority without self-promotion); relentless persistence and the repeated comeback; strategic clarity and tenacity (grasp the whole, keep pressing); magnanimity in victory (the generous Appomattox terms); using power for justice (Reconstruction, crushing the Klan); the honest man undone by misplaced trust (the Ferdinand Ward fraud · integrity is not competence in an unfamiliar domain); the honest reckoning with a lifelong struggle with drink.
- **Washington (Chernow · 7 + synthesis):** cultivated self-control (reserve as an instrument of authority); the self-invented public figure (the constructed self vs the inner man); the Fabian war of posts (survive by not losing, preserve the force); Cincinnatus (the deliberate relinquishing of power); coalition command under scarcity; setting precedents and the restraint of power; the flagrant contradiction (professed liberty vs slaveholding).
- **Synthesis (2):** the disciplined handling and relinquishing of power as the through-line (cross-source · `power`) + the leadership pattern library and the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

**No new domain · `character`/`statecraft`/`governance`/`politics`/`military`/`biography` deliberately NOT registered.** character routes to `leadership`/`ethics`/`operator-doctrine`; statecraft to `strategy`/`leadership`/`power`; governance to `strategy`/`operator-process`/`power`. `power` carries the most (4) because the material's dominant lesson is power-handling/relinquishment. `founder-psychology` and `systems-thinking` were available "if warranted" but not forced; `culture` (1) carries Washington's self-invention chunk.

**Distinguished from the founder/leadership canon.** Chernow's Titan (Rockefeller) lives in FOUNDER_SECOND_TIER (business-empire / founder-psychology / capital); Grant + Washington are military-and-political-leadership lives read through `leadership` / `power` / `strategy`. Net-new titles. This lane deepens the existing `leadership` domain (BATCH_002/003) into military + founding leadership.

**Curated, not exhaustive.** 16 chunks from ~912K combined words · representative leadership/power/operator patterns, not chapter-by-chapter coverage.

**Non-hagiographic.** Chunks 006 (Grant's misplaced trust / the Ward fraud), 007 (Grant's alcoholism), and 014 (Washington's slaveholding contradiction) hold achievement and failure in the same honest frame.

**Used the 2 Chernow sources only.** Titan (already in FOUNDER_SECOND_TIER) and the Bible contributed 0.

Chunk 016 makes the optionality discipline explicit.

Extracted via pdftotext + ebook-convert · no OCR · no new dependencies · ~597,690 words combined. **CURATED (representative), NOT exhaustive** per the scope guard. The FIRST lane of the sequenced CLASSICAL_STRATEGY_OPERATING_CANON.

The **curated classical strategy + operator-judgment canon**. It is the 27th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 8 domains pre-exist · `strategy` anchors. 18 chunks across: strategy (5), mindset (3), power (2), mental-models (2), operator-doctrine (2), leadership (2), ethics (1), operator-process (1).

**Signal captured:** power realism, judgment under uncertainty, friction and the gap between plan and execution, concentration of force, inner self-command, command discipline, the disciplined handling of power, and the difference between how power works and how one chooses to act.

**Source coverage (per-source attribution · Prince 4 / On War 5 / Meditations 5 / Caesar 4):**
- **The Prince (Machiavelli · 4):** feared vs loved (be feared without being hated); the fox and the lion (cunning + force); rely on your own arms not mercenaries; realpolitik read honestly (effects over intention) explicitly as analysis, NOT endorsement.
- **On War (Clausewitz · 4 + synthesis):** war as the continuation of politics by other means; friction ("everything is simple, but the simplest thing is difficult"); the center of gravity (concentrate on the decisive point); the culminating point + the strength of the defensive.
- **Meditations (Marcus Aurelius · 4 + synthesis):** the dichotomy of control; duty and the present; the inner citadel; memento mori. Read as SECULAR operator-discipline, NOT a faith lane.
- **Landmark Caesar (Strassler · 4):** command + calculated risk; clemency as strategy (clementia that made his position credible); control your own narrative (the Commentaries as self-account, named frankly as propaganda); the bond with his soldiers (loyalty through shared stake).
- **Synthesis (2):** the classical-strategy operating pattern (see clearly, concentrate force, govern yourself, act decisively with restraint · `strategy`) + the pattern-library discipline and the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

**No new domain · `philosophy`/`statecraft`/`war`/`history`/`politics`/`military`/`empire` deliberately NOT registered.** Stoicism routes to `mindset` / `operator-doctrine` / `ethics`; war-theory to `strategy` / `mental-models` / `leadership`; statecraft to `strategy` / `power`; Caesar's command to `leadership` / `strategy` / `operator-process`. `mental-models` (a thin domain at count 1) grows by 2 to 3. `decision-making` was an approved option but not forced.

This lane adds the Renaissance/modern/ancient classical-strategy texts BATCH_002 did not cover. Caesar's clemency chunk echoes Grant's Appomattox magnanimity (HISTORICAL_BIOGRAPHY) as a power-restraint pattern.

**Used the 4 curated sources only.** Book of Five Rings (djvu) excluded; Discourses, Napoleon, Herodotus, Thucydides, Arrian, Engels, and the Greene trio DEFERRED to subsequent classical lanes.

**CURRENT_OPERATOR_REALITY_BRIEF respected** (current-state anchor · NOT chunked doctrine · referenced in all 18 chunks).

**Identity optionality preserved:** all 18 chunks frame the texts as a strategy/power/operator pattern-library lens, **NOT doctrine and NOT a directive** · Machiavelli and Clausewitz are read for how power and conflict work, **explicitly NOT a mandate for ruthless power tactics** (the realpolitik chunk 004 is analysis, not endorsement) · **Meditations is read as SECULAR operator-discipline, NOT a faith lane** (the Bible was not touched) · this lane does NOT finalize SNIPED, SNIPED Media, or BASEPLATE direction · photography remains one option among several. Chunk 018 makes the optionality discipline explicit.

**Signal captured:** reading a culture from inside (nomos), the contingency of fortune, ambiguous signals read through hope, ownership as a force multiplier, the rising-power dynamic, power realism with its moral cost, strategic discipline against over-reach, catastrophic over-extension, how conflict corrupts a society, speed and tempo, leading from the front, decisive reframing, hubris and the failure of self-mastery, and the logistics that underwrite every bold move.

**Source coverage (per-source attribution · Herodotus 5 / Thucydides 6 / Arrian 4 / Engels 3):**
- **The Landmark Herodotus (4 + synthesis):** custom is king of all (read a scene's norms from inside · nomos); call no man fortunate until he is dead (the impermanence of fortune); the ambiguous oracle (Croesus heard "destroy a great empire" as he wished · hearing what you want to hear); free men outfight subjects (ownership as a force multiplier).
- **The Landmark Thucydides (5 + synthesis):** the real cause (the growth of Athens and the alarm it inspired in Sparta · the Thucydides Trap); the Melian dialogue (the strong do what they can · power realism read honestly, with the slaughter of Melos as its moral cost); Pericles' bounded strategy (attempt no new conquests · the discipline of not over-reaching); the Sicilian expedition (catastrophic over-extension far from base); stasis at Corcyra (conflict inverts values · words change their ordinary meaning).
- **The Campaigns of Alexander (Arrian · 4):** rapidity was all in all (speed and tempo as a weapon); leading from the front (the bond earned by visibly sharing risk); cutting the Gordian knot (refuse the inherited framing); hubris and the failure of self-mastery (insatiable appetite for fame · the Hyphasis limit · "self-restraint is a noble thing"), read as cautionary.
- **Alexander logistics (Engels · 3):** supply is the basis of strategy (logistics underwrites everything); the transport eats its own load (the carrying-capacity limit on reach); provisions arranged in advance (intelligence and preparation before the march).
- **Synthesis (2):** the ancient-history operating pattern (read the scene, see power clearly, hold discipline against over-reach, build the supply base first · `strategy`) + the pattern-library discipline and the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

**No new domain · `history`/`empire`/`war`/`politics`/`statecraft`/`military`/`civilization`/`antiquity` deliberately NOT registered.** Cultural-anthropology routes to `culture`; contingency/impermanence to `operator-doctrine`; ambiguous-signal cognition to `operator-process`; power realism + hubris to `power`; the rising-power dynamic + the Gordian-knot reframing to `mental-models`; logistics + preparation to `operator-process`; the carrying-capacity constraint to `systems-thinking`; corruption-of-values to `ethics`. `mental-models` (a thin domain at count 3) grows by 2 to 5, used sparingly; `systems-thinking` used once (squarely systemic).

**Distinguished from the already-canonical / deferred sources.** The Prince / On War / Meditations / Landmark Caesar are already chunked in CLASSICAL_STRATEGY and were EXCLUDED here; this lane illustrates those strategy treatises in lived events (Clausewitz's culminating point in the Sicilian and Hyphasis disasters; Machiavelli's power realism in the Melian dialogue). Art of War / 48 Laws / 33 Strategies are already in BATCH_002. Alexander's over-reach reads against Grant's/Washington's restraint (HISTORICAL_BIOGRAPHY).

**Used the 4 curated sources only.** Napoleon: A Life (Roberts) DEFERRED to a modern-command lane (HISTORICAL_BIOGRAPHY-adjacent); Discourses on Livy (Machiavelli) DEFERRED to a political-theory pass (CLASSICAL_STRATEGY-adjacent); Book of Five Rings (djvu) excluded.

**CURRENT_OPERATOR_REALITY_BRIEF respected** (current-state anchor · NOT chunked doctrine · referenced in all 18 chunks).

**Identity optionality preserved:** all 18 chunks frame the histories as a strategy/power/culture/operator pattern-library lens, **NOT doctrine and NOT a directive** · explicitly **NOT a mandate that BJ build an empire, seek political power, or copy ancient rulers**, and not an endorsement of conquest · the over-reach material (the Melian dialogue 006, the Sicilian expedition 008, Alexander's hubris 013) is read as **CAUTIONARY analysis, not aspiration** · the ethics chunk (009) keeps war's corrupting effect honest, not glorified · this lane does NOT finalize SNIPED, SNIPED Media, or BASEPLATE direction · photography remains one option among several. Chunk 018 makes the optionality discipline explicit.

**Bible excluded.** The KJV Bible was NOT touched, staged, chunked, or included · held separately as a SPIRITUAL_FOUNDATION anchor.

**Copyright-safe quote discipline:** 4 sources (the underlying ancient texts are public domain; the Landmark editions, the Arrian Penguin translation, and the Engels monograph are in-copyright editions/translations) · `direct_quotes` short illustrative lines only (longest 6 words) · extracted full text is INTERNAL chunk-authoring reference only.

**Cross-references opened to existing corpus:**

- **HISTORICAL_BIOGRAPHY:** the leadership/power-restraint through-line; Alexander's over-reach read against Grant/Washington's restraint; Napoleon: A Life is deferred to this register.
- **BATCH_002 (Tier-1 canon · Art of War, 48 Laws, 33 Strategies):** the existing strategy/power canon this lane complements (without re-chunking).

### MODERN_COMMAND_NAPOLEON · the curated modern-command case study mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/MODERN_COMMAND_NAPOLEON_CHUNKS.jsonl` (14 chunks · ~28 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-24
**Source corpus:** 1 curated modern-command biography from `raw/02_TIER_1_CANON_BOOKS/strategy_history/` (already repo-local) · Napoleon: A Life (Andrew Roberts · epub · 385,251 words). Extracted via ebook-convert · no OCR · no new dependencies. **CURATED (representative), NOT exhaustive** per the scope guard. The FIRST of the two deferred CLASSICAL_STRATEGY_OPERATING_CANON splits (the modern-command lane).

The **curated modern-command case study**. It is the 29th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 9 domains pre-exist · `leadership` anchors. 14 chunks across: leadership (4), operator-process (3), strategy (1), founder-psychology (1), culture (1), systems-thinking (1), power (1), ethics (1), operator-doctrine (1).

**Signal captured:** the leadership craft (inspiring belief, leading from the front, motivation by recognition, energy and compartmentalized focus), the operator machine (command of detail, logistics, durable institution-building), the operational art (concentration on the decisive point), the self-made outsider arc, self-myth and narrative control, and the cautionary collapse (the self-defeating Continental System, the appetite that would not relinquish, the over-reach into Russia, the honest human cost).

**Source coverage (single source · Napoleon: A Life · 14 chunks incl 1 synthesis):**
- **Leadership (4):** make ordinary people feel capable of extraordinary deeds (the army followed him after Moscow); leading from the front (le petit caporal, shared risk); lead by recognition ("it is with such baubles that men are led", the Legion d'Honneur); energy and compartmentalized focus ("drawers in a cupboard").
- **Operator-process (3):** command of detail ("one of the most unrelenting micromanagers in history"); logistics and supply discipline ("three minutes without gunpowder", an army marches on its feet); build durable institutions (the Code Napoleon · "My true glory ... is my Civil Code" · "Is this fair? Is this useful?").
- **Strategy (1):** the operational art, concentrate force on the decisive point ("attacks must not be disseminated, but concentrated"), the spine Clausewitz later theorized.
- **Founder-psychology (1):** the self-made Corsican outsider / autodidact ("read constantly, especially history books") who reinvented himself and embodied careers-open-to-talent.
- **Culture (1):** self-myth and narrative control, the bulletins (he "didn't consider himself to be on oath"), the St Helena memoir, echoing Caesar's Commentaries.
- **Systems-thinking (1):** the Continental System as a self-defeating policy that boomeranged ("protectionism that brought him down", his own army in "uniforms made in Halifax and Leeds").
- **Power (1):** the appetite that would not relinquish, the Cincinnatus pose vs Washington's actual relinquishment ("more glory than is necessary"), "insatiable hubris", Russia 1812 as the culminating point. Cautionary.
- **Ethics (1):** the human cost, read honestly, the wars and the dead, the "unprecedentedly efficient surveillance system" and censorship, the 1802 reintroduction of slavery. Cautionary, not glorified.
- **Synthesis (1):** the modern-command operating pattern (energy + system + tempo + recognition + durable institutions) + the over-reach counter-lesson + the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

**No new domain · `military`/`politics`/`empire`/`conquest`/`biography`/`history`/`commander` deliberately NOT registered.** Military-command routes to `leadership` / `strategy`; politics to `power` / `strategy`; empire / conquest to `power` (cautionary); biography to `leadership` / `founder-psychology`; administration to `operator-process`; the Continental System to `systems-thinking`; the moral cost to `ethics`. `mental-models` was available but not used (the over-reach routed to `power` + `systems-thinking`).

**Distinguished from the already-canonical / deferred sources.** Grant + Washington: A Life (Chernow) are already chunked in HISTORICAL_BIOGRAPHY and were EXCLUDED; Napoleon is the cautionary counter-case to their power-restraint (he never relinquished; the over-reach destroyed him). The Prince / On War / Meditations / Landmark Caesar are already CLASSICAL_STRATEGY (Clausewitz literally theorized Napoleonic war; this is the lived case). Herodotus / Thucydides / Arrian / Engels are already CLASSICAL_HISTORY (Alexander's hubris and the Sicilian expedition are the ancient mirror of Russia 1812). Art of War / 48 Laws / 33 Strategies are already BATCH_002.

**Used the 1 curated source only.** Discourses on Livy (Machiavelli) DEFERRED to a political-theory pass (CLASSICAL_STRATEGY-adjacent · the second split); Book of Five Rings (djvu) excluded.

**CURRENT_OPERATOR_REALITY_BRIEF respected** (current-state anchor · NOT chunked doctrine · referenced in all 14 chunks).

### POLITICAL_THEORY_DISCOURSES · the curated institutional-power case study mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/POLITICAL_THEORY_DISCOURSES_CHUNKS.jsonl` (12 chunks · ~24 KB)

Extracted via pdftotext · no OCR · no new dependencies. **CURATED (representative), NOT exhaustive** per the scope guard. The SECOND (and final) of the two deferred CLASSICAL_STRATEGY_OPERATING_CANON splits (the political-theory lane); closes the classical block.

The **curated institutional-power case study**. It is the 30th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 8 domains pre-exist · `power` anchors. 12 chunks across: power (3), operator-doctrine (2), strategy (2), operator-process (1), leadership (1), culture (1), systems-thinking (1), ethics (1).

**Signal captured:** how channeled conflict produces good laws, the balance of the few against the many, how liberty corrupts through ambition and inequality, the lawful release valve of accusation vs calumny, periodic renewal back to first beginnings, the founder who must act alone before handing off to institutions, adaptation to the times, the asymmetric risk of conspiracies, shared belief as cohesion, institutional decay as a compounding system, and ends-and-means realism read honestly.

**Source coverage (single source · Discourses on Livy · 12 chunks incl 1 synthesis):**
- **Power (3):** healthy conflict makes good laws (the Roman tumults · "wise laws in these very tumults"); a people is "wiser and more constant than a prince" (the broad base as guardian of liberty); how liberty is lost to ambition, inequality, and the idle gentry ("to live as gentlemen").
- **Operator-process (1):** the right to accuse vs calumny ("the right to accuse" as a lawful release valve; "Calumny is as hurtful" when grievance has no accountable outlet).
- **Operator-doctrine (2):** return to first beginnings ("back to their first beginnings" · periodic renewal before drift compounds); the synthesis (the institution-design operating pattern + the optionality guardrail).
- **Leadership (1):** the founder "must stand alone" to establish order, then hand off to institutions (founding and sustaining demand opposite things).
- **Strategy (2):** adapt to the times or fall ("he does not change with them"); conspiracies usually fail ("danger at three stages" · asymmetric downside of covert schemes).
- **Culture (1):** shared belief as cohesion ("making use of religion to keep the minds" · instrumental, honest).
- **Systems-thinking (1):** institutions decay without renewal ("in progress of time this excellence" corrupts · compounding entropy).
- **Ethics (1):** ends-and-means realism read honestly ("excused rather than blamed" · analysis of how founding power behaves, explicitly NOT an endorsement of cruelty or manipulation).

**No new domain · `politics`/`political-theory`/`republic`/`statecraft`/`governance`/`history`/`empire` deliberately NOT registered.** Political-theory / republic / governance / statecraft route to `power` / `operator-doctrine` / `strategy`; institutional design to `operator-process` / `power`; the Livy/Rome history to `culture`; the corruption dynamic to `systems-thinking`; the realpolitik to `ethics`. Mirrors the CLASSICAL_STRATEGY routing. `mental-models` was available but not used (the regime-decay dynamic routed to `systems-thinking`). `power` (a thinner domain at count 22) is the anchor this lane legitimately deepens (grows 22 to 25).

**Distinguished from the already-canonical / deferred sources.** The Prince is already CLASSICAL_STRATEGY and was EXCLUDED; the Discourses is the complementary republican-strategy treatise by the same author (the prince who founds vs the institutions that endure), closing the Machiavelli pair. On War / Meditations / Landmark Caesar are already CLASSICAL_STRATEGY; Herodotus / Thucydides / Arrian / Engels are already CLASSICAL_HISTORY (the Discourses comments on Livy's Rome, the same world); Napoleon: A Life is already MODERN_COMMAND_NAPOLEON; Art of War / 48 Laws / 33 Strategies are already BATCH_002.

**Used the 1 curated source only.** The Book of Five Rings (djvu) excluded; the Greene trio (Laws of Human Nature / Mastery / 50th Law) is a separate Tier-2-style cluster, NOT bundled.

**CURRENT_OPERATOR_REALITY_BRIEF respected** (current-state anchor · NOT chunked doctrine · referenced in all 12 chunks).

The **curated operating-founder startup toolkit**. It is the 31st mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 7 domains pre-exist · `operator-doctrine` anchors. 15 chunks across: operator-doctrine (4), founder-psychology (3), operator-process (2), strategy (2), leadership (2), commercial-architecture (1), ethics (1).

**Signal captured:** validated learning, the MVP + build-measure-learn loop, pivot-or-persevere, the engine of growth (Ries); the Struggle, wartime vs peacetime CEO, people-products-profits, tell it like it is, lead bullets (Horowitz); Rich vs King, the Three Rs, equity splits, solo-vs-team and when-to-found, founder optimism bias (Wasserman).

**Source coverage (per-source 5/5/5 · incl 1 synthesis):**
- **The Lean Startup (Ries · 4 + synthesis):** validated learning (progress = learning, not output); the minimum viable product + build-measure-learn loop (actionable not vanity metrics); pivot or persevere (the evidence-based strategic inflection); the engine of growth (sticky/viral/paid · tune its governing variables).
- **The Hard Thing About Hard Things (Horowitz · 5):** the Struggle (focus when there are "no good moves"); wartime vs peacetime CEO (match the mode to the moment); take care of "the people, the products, and the profits, in that order" + the right kind of ambition; CEOs should "tell it like it is"; "only lead bullets" / "no silver bullets" (no shortcut to a better product).
- **The Founder's Dilemmas (Wasserman · 5):** Rich versus King (the wealth-versus-control trade-off); the Three Rs (relationships, roles, and rewards); equity splits and the cost of the quick handshake (early structure compounds); solo vs team and when to found (career, market, and personal factors); founders' biases (passion, optimism, and conflict avoidance breed shortsighted decisions).
- **Synthesis (015):** the build-under-uncertainty toolkit (learn fast, decide on evidence, lead with candor and hard work, structure partnerships deliberately, know your Rich-vs-King stance) + the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

**No new domain · `startup`/`entrepreneurship`/`founder`/`business`/`operations`/`scaling`/`product-development` deliberately NOT registered or used.** Startup/entrepreneurship method routes to `operator-doctrine` / `operator-process` / `strategy`; founder reality to `founder-psychology` / `leadership`; business-model to `commercial-architecture`; MVP / product-iteration to `operator-process` / `strategy` (NOT a `product-development` domain). `operations` and `scaling` already exist in the corpus but were deliberately NOT used or grown by this lane. `systems-thinking` and `distribution` were available but not used (the build-measure-learn loop routed to `operator-process`; the distribution source, Traction, is broken/deferred).

The **curated operations/operator-system toolkit**. **Signal captured:** the goal and its three measures (throughput/inventory/operational-expense), dependent events + statistical fluctuations, the constraint that governs throughput, the five focusing steps (The Goal); fundamental rethinking + radical redesign, process orientation, the clean-sheet test (Reengineering); work ON not IN the business, the Fatal Assumption, the franchise prototype (E-Myth); owner-independence, specialize/productize, recurring revenue (Built to Sell).

**Source coverage (per-source The Goal 5 / Reengineering 3 / E-Myth 3 / Built to Sell 3 · incl 1 synthesis):**
- **The Goal (Goldratt · 4 + synthesis):** know the goal and its measures ("the goal is to make money" · throughput, inventory, operational expense); dependent events + statistical fluctuations (the chain underperforms its parts); the constraint governs throughput ("an hour lost at a bottleneck"); the five focusing steps (identify, exploit, subordinate, elevate, repeat).
- **Reengineering the Corporation (Hammer/Champy · 3):** "fundamental rethinking and radical redesign" for dramatic (not incremental) improvement; process orientation ("organized around outcomes" · "Don't Automate, Obliterate"); the clean-sheet test (redesign from the outcome backward).
- **The E-Myth Revisited (Gerber · 3):** work ON your business, not IN it ("Working On Your Business" · "rather than in it"); the Fatal Assumption (being good at the craft is not running the business · Technician/Manager/Entrepreneur); the franchise prototype (build it as if it must run without you · a thinking tool, NOT a directive to franchise).
- **Built to Sell (Warrillow · 3):** owner-independence (a job vs an asset · runs without you); specialize and productize (one teachable, repeatable service); recurring revenue on a standardized process.
- **Synthesis (014):** the operations toolkit (find the constraint, redesign from the outcome backward, work ON the system, build owner-independence and recurring revenue) + the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

### NETWORK_DISTRIBUTION · the curated network/distribution-economics toolkit mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/NETWORK_DISTRIBUTION_CHUNKS.jsonl` (16 chunks · ~28 KB)
**Date processed:** 2026-05-25
**Source corpus:** 5 curated net-new sources from `raw/02_TIER_1_CANON_BOOKS/network_distribution/` (already repo-local) · The Inevitable (Kevin Kelly · epub · 108,050 words); New Rules for the New Economy (Kevin Kelly · pdf · 61,078 words); The Long Tail (Chris Anderson · epub · 75,305 words); Free: The Future of a Radical Price (Chris Anderson · pdf · 74,656 words); The Great Online Game (Packy McCormick · pdf · 4,343 words). Extracted via ebook-convert + pdftotext · no OCR · no new dependencies · ~323,432 words combined. **CURATED (representative), NOT exhaustive** per the scope guard.

The **curated network/distribution-economics toolkit**. It is the 34th mini-batch and introduces **NO new domain** · all 8 domains pre-exist · `distribution` anchors. 16 chunks across: distribution (5), strategy (3), network-effects (2), commercial-architecture (2), systems-thinking (1), media-business (1), status (1), operator-doctrine (1).

**Signal captured:** the 12 technological forces as trajectories, flowing and the un-copyable generatives, accessing and sharing, filtering and attention-scarcity (The Inevitable); increasing returns and the law of plenitude, follow the free, opportunities not efficiencies (New Rules); selling less of more, the three forces, hits-to-niches and filters as tastemakers, make-everything-available-and-help-me-find-it (The Long Tail); the economics of abundance and cross-subsidy, free as a distribution strategy buying attention and reputation, free as a conditional not a default (Free); building in public compounds status into opportunity (The Great Online Game).

**Source coverage (per-source The Inevitable 5 / The Long Tail 4 / New Rules 3 / Free 3 / The Great Online Game 1 · incl 1 synthesis):**
- **The Inevitable (Kelly · 4 + synthesis):** the 12 forces are trajectories not products; flowing (free copies push value to the generatives: immediacy, authenticity, embodiment, patronage); accessing and sharing (access beats ownership, cooperation compounds); filtering ("attention is the" scarce resource in abundance).
- **New Rules for the New Economy (Kelly · 3):** increasing returns + the law of plenitude (value explodes with membership); "Follow the Free" (generosity as a distribution engine); "opportunities, not efficiencies" / "Let Go at the Top."
- **The Long Tail (Anderson · 4):** "selling less of more" (aggregated niches rival the head); the three forces (democratize production, democratize distribution, connect supply and demand); the tyranny of the hit ends, filters become the new tastemakers; "make everything available" + "help me find it."
- **Free (Anderson · 3):** the economics of abundance ("marginal cost" toward zero) and "cross-subsidies" (the kinds of free); free buys "attention and reputation" (the gift economy); "free is not" a universal answer, "waste is good" only with a paid layer (conditional).
- **The Great Online Game (McCormick · 1):** "The Great Online Game" / "Win the Internet" · building in public compounds reputation and status into real-world opportunity.
- **Synthesis (016):** the connected-economy distribution toolkit (charge for the un-copyable, give away to earn attention/reputation, design free as cross-subsidy, aggregate niches with navigation, let public work compound) + the optionality guardrail (`operator-doctrine`).

**No new domain · `network`/`network-effect`(singular)/`platform`/`platform-strategy`/`growth`/`marketing`/`startup`/`business`/`economics` deliberately NOT registered or used.** The abundance/long-tail/free distribution material routes to `distribution`; the radical strategies + long-tail rule + free-conditional to `strategy`; increasing-returns + access/sharing compounding to `network-effects`; the three forces + freemium/cross-subsidy to `commercial-architecture`; the 12 technological forces to `systems-thinking`; the niche-content/filters to `media-business`; the online-game build-in-public to `status`; the synthesis to `operator-doctrine`. **The EXISTING `network-effects` (plural · count 5 from BATCH_002's The Cold Start Problem) was REUSED (grows 5 to 7), NOT created; the forbidden singular `network-effect` is a different string and was NOT created.** `culture`, `operator-process`, and `ethics` were available but not used.

**Distinguished from the already-canonical / deferred sources.** The Cold Start Problem (Andrew Chen) is ALREADY chunked in BATCH_002 (`network-effects`) and was EXCLUDED, not re-chunked. Traction (0-byte broken epub in `operating_founder/`) is deferred to recovery (a future tactical-distribution-channels addendum). The SNIPED-authored network/platform docs (13_NETWORK access_and_community md, The_Platform_Stack.docx, platform-ranking.md) are out-of-scope operational/identity-side docs.

**Used the 5 curated sources only.** The Cold Start Problem (already BATCH_002), broken Traction, and the SNIPED-authored docs excluded.

### EXPERTISE_CREATIVITY · the curated expertise/creative-skill operator toolkit mini-batch
**File:** `01_KNOWLEDGE_BASE/batches/EXPERTISE_CREATIVITY_CHUNKS.jsonl` (11 chunks · ~19 KB)

Extracted via pdftotext + ebook-convert · no OCR · no new dependencies · ~254,243 words combined. The Dieter Rams image-only monograph (0 extractable text) and the Csikszentmihalyi djvu (unsupported) were excluded. **CURATED (representative), NOT exhaustive** per the scope guard. ## Source families (9 families now)

### Family 1 · SNIPED OS docs (BATCH_001 + BATCH_004, ~200 chunks)
The user's own SNIPED operating documents. BATCH_001 covered 25 best-first files; BATCH_004 completed coverage of the previously-sampled or deferred files.

### Family 2 · Strategy canon (BATCH_002, ~80 chunks)
Sun Tzu, Greene ×2, Thiel, Thorndike, Munger, Weatherford, Freeman, Bezos/Stone.

### Family 3 · Operator memoirs (BATCH_002, ~50 chunks)
Isaacson (Jobs), Iger, Knight, Catmull, Bryar/Carr (Amazon), Stewart (Disney).

### Family 4 · Brand + culture canon (BATCH_002 + BATCH_003, ~40 chunks combined)
Stoute ×2, Chen, Seabrook (B2); Holiday, Elberse, Sax (B3 brand/culture sub-cluster).

### Family 5 · Operating-doctrine canon (BATCH_003 dominant, ~85 chunks)
Enns ×2 (pricing + sales-flow), Guidara (hospitality), de Botton + Simler/Hanson (status + signaling), Naval + Jarvis (leverage + small-company strategy).

### Family 6 · Photography canon + SNIPED Art Series (BATCH_005, 161 chunks)
Canonical theory (Sontag, Barthes, Day, Shore, Szarkowski, Freeman ×2, Stevens-Avedon, Maisel · 86 chunks), the 9-photographer SNIPED Art Series doctrine (9 production plans + 9 Studies + 1 wrapper · 65 chunks), Abloh Core Studio lecture (3 chunks), and GOLD-vault scholarly extracts (Dubiel, Talbot, Art Book 1994 · 7 chunks). The first deep photography corpus in the SNIPED brain.

### Family 7 · Operator-engine skill layer (BATCH_006, 114 chunks)
The reusable execution layer · 50 SNIPED skill packs + 1 SKILL_BUILD_QUEUE meta-doc + 50 external framework prompts (TCREI, premortem, fermi, Munger 2-track, signal-noise-bayesian, framework-orchestrator, et al.) + 4 Claude / AI tool workflows (Claude Code Superpowers, Plugin / Ralph Wiggum, AI Ops Dashboard PRD, Built an AI SaaS in 20 min) + 1 video automation primitive (Remotion) + 2 directly-executable n8n automation blueprints (30-day content strategy generator, ElevenLabs voice agent). The operator engine made self-describing. Not an AI-course dump · every chunk strengthens SNIPED workflow execution.

### Family 8 · Locked doctrine + SOPs + working drafts + operator docs (BATCH_007, 128 chunks)
The doctrine + SOP source-of-truth layer that BATCH_006's skill layer reads at invocation. 14 locked-doctrine docs (12 canonical truths, THE SPINE, lineage doctrine, operating locks, operator-coded definition, lean execution audit, Monday cockpit, Saturday build brief, system final status, operator-questions catalog, partnership protocol, recurring checklists, 2 templates · 37 chunks). 13 production SOPs (casting call, chapter intake, Lightroom OS with v3 LUXURY supersession, retoucher training, capture-to-delivery, Reset shoot day, strategic-free, Track B walkthrough, composite environment rotation, preset library, 2 checklists, ch02 brief · 29 chunks). 7 outreach SOPs (LinkedIn comment doctrine, the canonical v3 assistant SOP, discovery call, discovery-to-close, VIB production + caption library + Figma spec · 16 chunks). 11 delivery docs (SOP_post_delivery + Pixieset config + 9 email templates · 13 chunks). 7 content docs (philosophy, video philosophy, audience engine, caption templates, cultural-documentation thesis, hook library, POV bank · 26 chunks). 3 commercial / network singletons (delivery_architecture_v2, notion CRM schemas, access + community architecture · 7 chunks). 4 STALE-FLAG chunks preserve the BATCH_004 SYNTHESIS audit trail.

### Family 9 · Operator-doctrine cultural-canon extensions (mini-batches, 16 chunks so far)
Cultural-canon backing layer for previously operator-only frames. **INTELLECTUAL_ARTIST_FRAME** (7 chunks · MJ Moonwalk · DESCRIPTIVE · disciplined-time + cross-domain study + performer-operator lineage + obsessive craft + stagecraft + depth-over-churn + movement composition). **PERSONAL_OPERATING_CODE** (9 chunks · 88 Laws · John Winters · PRESCRIPTIVE · ownership + discipline + mission obsession + code + time control + consistency + execution + composure + mindset-as-software · gender-war/fitness/nutrition/dating/body-image material excluded). The two mini-batches form a descriptive + prescriptive pair on the same operator-doctrine cluster. Future candidates: Quincy Jones autobiography, Miles Davis autobiography, Pressfield War of Art, Aurelius Meditations, Goggins Can't Hurt Me, Willink Discipline Equals Freedom · operator-curated additions.

The mini-batch naming convention (`<NAME>` instead of `BATCH_NNN`) preserves numbered-batch slots for the originally-planned batches while letting single-source cultural-canon additions land cleanly. Each mini-batch reuses existing domains (no NEW domain per addition); cumulative domain counts roll up into the main `combined_domain_counts` aggregate.

**No conflicts.** BATCH_007 closes the source-of-truth gap that BATCH_006's skill layer references at invocation. 2-hop retrieval (B6 skill chunk → B7 doctrine doc) now works end-to-end. The 4 STALE-FLAG chunks in BATCH_007 preserve the BATCH_004 SYNTHESIS audit trail · retrieval should prefer the B4 resolutions while keeping the source-doc trail intact for future sweep work. The locked SNIPED operating discipline (canonical truths, the 10 operating locks, lineage doctrine, partnership protocol, BASEPLATE firewall, Year-10 4-7 person team, 65+ named refusals) is now chunk-addressable in primary-source form (B7) on top of the skill-layer invocation paths (B6) and the strategic substrate (B2-B4).

### STALE-FLAG governance (BATCH_007)

4 chunks carry STALE-FLAG tags that preserve the BATCH_004 SYNTHESIS audit trail. Each tag identifies legacy language in a source doc that has been superseded by a B4 resolution, with the doc itself not yet swept.

| chunk_id | source_file | tag | canonical resolution |
|---|---|---|---|
| BATCH_007_054 | `production__lightroom_operating_system.md` | `legacy-adobe-portrait-pending-sweep` | v3 LUXURY · Adobe Neutral (NOT Adobe Portrait / Camera Standard / HSL orange punch). Doc supersession header at lines 7-17 is canonical; body refs are pending sweep. B4 SYNTHESIS Contradiction 2. |
| BATCH_007_070 | `outreach__sop_assistant.md` | `stale-phase-b-trigger-3k-vs-2k` | Phase B trigger = $2K MRR x 3 months (NOT $3K x 2). B4 100Q recalibration. |
| BATCH_007_111 | `content__sniped_content_philosophy.md` | `legacy-language-sweep-pending` | B4 SYNTHESIS Contradiction 5 audit trail. Doc currently reads hybrid-aligned ("AI cannot commodify in 5 years") rather than absolute anti-AI as B4 flagged. Tag preserved for future audit. |
| BATCH_007_122 | `offers__delivery_architecture_v2.md` | `stale-hero-count-8-vs-10-12` | Hero count = 10-12 (NOT 8-12). Doc shows 8-12 range; B4 SYNTHESIS Contradiction 4 resolved canonical to 10-12 (8 floor is legacy). |

**Retrieval should de-prioritize these 4 chunks** in favor of the B4 SYNTHESIS resolutions when answering questions about Adobe profiles, Phase B triggers, AI-stance language, or Hero counts. Source docs need future sweep work to remove the legacy phrasing.

### Schema heterogeneity note · CRITICAL for retrieval

The seven batches use **three different chunk schemas**. Any retrieval system MUST handle all three:

| Batch | ID field | Batch field | Content fields | Schema notes |
|-------|----------|-------------|----------------|--------------|
| BATCH_001 | `id` | (none) | `content` (single field) | Older schema; topic, source, priority, tags |
| BATCH_002 | `chunk_id` | `batch` | `summary` + `usable_principle` + `sniped_relevance` + `direct_quotes` | Richer schema; source_title, source_file, author, concept |
| BATCH_003 | `chunk_id` | `batch_id` | (same as B2) | `batch_id` not `batch` |
| BATCH_004 | `chunk_id` | `batch_id` | (same as B3) | Same schema as B3 · canonical going forward |
| BATCH_005 | `chunk_id` | `batch_id` | (same as B3) | Same schema as B3/B4 · canonical |
| BATCH_006 | `chunk_id` | `batch_id` | (same as B3) | Same schema as B3/B4/B5 · canonical |
| BATCH_007 | `chunk_id` | `batch_id` | (same as B3) | Same schema as B3/B4/B5/B6 · canonical |

**All seven batches share:** `domain` (string), `tags` (array). These are the safest fields for filter-based retrieval.

**Retrieval normalization rules:**
1. **ID:** treat `id` (B1) and `chunk_id` (B2-B7) as canonical. Pattern matches `batch-NNN-chunk-NNN` or `BATCH_NNN_NNN` for B5/B6/B7.
2. **Batch:** derive batch from ID prefix or from the `batch_id` field.
3. **Content:** for embedding/indexing, concatenate `topic + content` (B1) or `concept + summary + usable_principle + sniped_relevance` (B2-B7).
4. **Domain + tags work uniformly** across all seven.
5. **Future batches MUST use BATCH_003-B7 schema** (`chunk_id` + `batch_id` + 12-field structure).
6. **STALE-FLAG tags from BATCH_007** (`legacy-adobe-portrait-pending-sweep`, `stale-hero-count-8-vs-10-12`, `stale-phase-b-trigger-3k-vs-2k`, `legacy-language-sweep-pending`) signal de-prioritization in favor of B4 SYNTHESIS resolutions.
