## Recommended retrieval patterns

1. **Default ranked retrieval** · Query embeddings against all seven JSONLs, dedupe by topic similarity, rank by domain-relevance score. Use normalized content fields (see schema heterogeneity note). De-prioritize chunks tagged with STALE-FLAG (4 in B7) in favor of B4 SYNTHESIS resolutions.
2. **Domain-filtered retrieval** · Filter by `domain` field first (works uniformly across batches), then rank within domain. Use the domain map above to choose the right batch primary.
3. **Tag-filtered retrieval** · Union of tag sets across all batches.
4. **Author-filtered retrieval** (BATCH_002 through BATCH_007) · When wanting one specific thinker's perspective. BATCH_007 author is uniformly "BJ / SNIPED Media" because all 55 source docs are SNIPED-internal doctrine.
5. **Decision-ready retrieval** · For B2-B7 chunks, prioritize the `sniped_relevance` field · it pre-translates general principles into SNIPED's specific context.
6. **2-hop B6→B7 retrieval** · For any SNIPED skill question, pull the B6 skill chunk first (invocation pattern + INVOKE-WHEN condition), then follow MANDATORY READING references to the B7 source-doc chunk (the doctrine the skill reads at runtime).

### When to prioritize BATCH_004 specifically
- Question is "what did BJ already decide about X?" → BATCH_004 (operational-locks, decision-archaeology)
- Question is about SNIPED's locked aesthetic direction → BATCH_004 (aesthetic-doctrine)
- Question requires the cross-source integrated paragraph or 9-factor decomposition → BATCH_004 (doctrine-meta)
- Question is about Meta infrastructure (Business Portfolio, 2FA, Lead Center, ads) → BATCH_004 (meta-architecture)
- Question is about offer mechanics (Sprint guardrail, upsell paths, Reputation Engine 4-lane) → BATCH_004 (commercial-architecture)
- Question is about the 2026 win conditions, BASEPLATE firewall, or 7/30/90-day ladder → BATCH_004 (operational-locks)
- Question is about brand positioning frameworks (us-vs-them, identity bridge, marketing vector, content-is-product) → BATCH_004 (brand-psychology)
- Question is about SNIPED's named moat surfaces → BATCH_004 (moat-surfaces)
- Question requires authentic BJ voice register or thinking origin → BATCH_004 (decision-archaeology)

### When to prioritize BATCH_005 specifically
- Question is about the Direction Stack book's photography-canon chapter → BATCH_005 (photography-theory, visual-literacy, composition)
- Question is "how does Avedon / Eggleston / Frank / Herzog / Shore / Meyerowitz / Leibovitz / Iturbide / Haas do X?" → BATCH_005 (art-series · Study + Art_Series production plan)
- Question requires primary-source anti-AI defense → BATCH_005 (Sontag appropriation, Barthes noeme, Abloh 3% rule)
- Question is about the v3 LUXURY palette's theoretical defense → BATCH_005 (Szarkowski color legitimation, Talbot Herzog modern color, Maisel three things)
- Question is about the 5-pass cull vocabulary → BATCH_005 (Shore three-level analysis)
- Question is about the apparatus principle (studio as the work) → BATCH_005 (Stevens-Avedon)
- Question is about the personal documentary lineage (Cultural Doc lane) → BATCH_005 (Day · Frank's *The Americans*)
- Question is about the 90-second opener / body architecture / portraiture direction → BATCH_005 (Stevens, Barthes 4-poses, Dubiel-Sartre)
- Question is about the monograph form / perennial-seller validation in photography → BATCH_005 (Art Book 1994, Day on 30-year canonization)
- Question is about photographer ethics or the operator's obligation to the subject → BATCH_005 (Sontag · non-intervention, ethics domain)

## SUPPLEMENTAL BATCHES · Promotion Batch 1 · 2026-05-31 (missed-gold closure)

After a missed-gold audit proved the corpus was chain-of-custody complete but not principle-complete, three supplemental mini-batches were consolidated (1837 to 1879 chunks · 47 to 50 mini-batches). All identity-neutral (reference both SNIPED and BASEPLATE, crown neither), no sensitive or expert-gated content.

**DECISION_SYSTEMS_SUPP** (48th supplemental · 18 chunks · `DECISION_SYSTEMS_SUPP_CHUNKS.jsonl`). Transferable decision-system method abstracted from BJ's personal analytics / betting / reselling corpus. NO picks, odds, teams, or personal numbers were chunked. New domains `decision-systems` (12) and `money-systems` (4) introduced; `toolchain` grows. Covers: EV gate, mispricing-as-edge, floor-over-ceiling variance control, stake-to-variance-not-conviction, scan-first verification, kill-conditions, analyst-seat vs decider-seat separation, survival filter, hard-constraint gate, outside-view base rates, LLM-advisor-vs-realtime-data split, ground-truth ingestion, tiered calibrated output, removed-constraint vacuum, fully-loaded unit economics, U-shaped fee curve, liquidity/80-20 tail, effective-hourly-wage make-vs-delegate.

**TOOLCHAIN_DISTRIBUTION_SUPP** (49th supplemental · 12 chunks · `TOOLCHAIN_DISTRIBUTION_SUPP_CHUNKS.jsonl`). From Claude Code subagents, GaryVee keynote, Super Carl/OpenAI API masterclass, and the Once Upon a Time editorial edit. New domains `toolchain` and `distribution`. Covers: scoped-specialist delegation, task-dependency-graph coordination, adversarial multi-agent convergence; interest-media-over-social, creative-first sequencing, underpriced-attention arbitrage; conversion-page skeleton, negative positioning, metered pricing ladder; swipe-file-to-concept fusion, attention-hierarchy editing, concept-before-capture.

**LEADERSHIP_SUPP** (50th supplemental · 12 chunks · `LEADERSHIP_SUPP_CHUNKS.jsonl`). ADDITIVE DEPTH, not void-filling. NOTE: Team of Rivals, Leadership in Turbulent Times, and The Dichotomy of Leadership were ALREADY curated in LEADERSHIP_MGMT (45th mini-batch, representative 1-chunk-each). This supplemental deepens them (3 chunks per book on distinct principles) plus deepens four 1-chunk decision-framework prompts (Munger two-track, Superforecasting, 7 Powers + 3-S filter, Strategy Kernel). A future dedup review against LEADERSHIP_MGMT is optional. Grows leadership (6), strategy (2), meta-doctrine (2), founder-psychology (1), operator-process (1).

These three supplemental batches close the missed-gold gap (transferable-principle capture raised from ~78% to ~91%). The remaining residual is OCR/visual and expert-gated (legitimately deferred). Identity optionality preserved throughout · these are decision-neutral operator tooling, NOT a directive and NOT a finalization of SNIPED / BASEPLATE direction.
